CORRECTION (2026-10-28): the earlier OCR + roster-tagged keyword-candidate pass is NOT a hand read. Status now counts only pages logged as "hand-read (full text)" — the full OCR text of each page read by the model, with no keyword/roster pre-filter — plus the core books hand-read earlier (CORE). "Scan-only pages" = covered only by the earlier keyword pass and still needing the full read.

Older notes: method varies by book and is stated in each Notes cell. Items 7-13 style full hand reads are marked "hand-read"; most D20 adventures/magazines and all Insider issues were covered by OCR of every page plus a roster-tagged scan for power/feat/ranking sentences and stat blocks, with every resulting candidate (and every previously dropped auto-candidate) reviewed by hand — that is a full-page scan, not a line-by-line human read. "Hand-read pages" lists the file-page ranges covered and logged; quotes/stat notes/summaries are counts of hand records added. Page numbers are file pages (printed page + 1 for most books).

It recalls 'Eyes of the Sun' at WF2001 (the Metatheran Cartel and E1-6RA), ten game years on with the galaxy at war, and the 'Forces of Cularin' trilogy by Matthew Domville (Force Concession, Contention, Convention) plus the interactive 'Force Conflagration'. Dariana, Mother of the Hiironi and Liriana's younger sister, records electronic memoirs, breaking with oral tradition. The editor's notes name the Tarasin head-fan 'kampo' and the forearm quills 'sa'tosin' for the first time. Her mother says 'Cularin chose us'; the Jedi 'luminous beings'; her vision of the Force as a web; and the young kilassin she killed with a dark Force grip; pp1-2 |

Yara Grugara flies to the Militia training city on Genarius despite restricted airspace; Wookiees block the ramp; a droid photobombs her shot; then an interview with Broof Yurdel, the Gungan commander of the Militia's ground divisions, covering the Thaereian Navy, the Clone War, a donation plug and the missing Nim'Ri, who recently resigned from House Hirskaala. p5 is just the LF intro line, read from the page image; pp1-5 |

Felanil Baaks will train five Jedi Knights (not Padawans) as artisans. Requirements: BAB +5; Craft (lightsaber) 10 ranks; Knowledge (Jedi lore) 8; Force-Sensitive, Alter, Control, Sense; must have crafted own lightsaber and have a mentor; Jedi levels. VP 1d6; 6+Int skill points. Features: Skill Emphasis (Craft [lightsaber]); mastercraft lightsaber +1/+2/+3 (DC 25/30); deflect; increase lightsaber damage +1d8; lightsaber mastery (Weapon Focus or Weapon Finesse); material mastery (example: Jwartek the Ithorian's branch casing). Background: a junior Senator without a system during Cularin's disappearance (the Jedi felt it as ten million people entering hyperspace and not coming out). The speech, given beside the statue of Reidi Artom in Gadrin: the 'small ship' metaphor; Almas as a possible Separatist target; Geonosis showed Jedi are mortal; Thaere's navy as protector. Thaereian troops drag off three Humans shouting 'Down with the Supreme Chancellor!' She closes loyal to Palpatine: 'Long live the Republic!' pp1-2 |
| d433 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/05:13:03 - Jedi Artisan Contest.pdf | 2 | 1-2 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 2 | 0 | 2 | 1-2: 05:13:03 - Jedi Artisan Contest (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-2: hand-read (full text): Living Force 'Jedi Artisan Contest': an application procedure for five heroes to train under Felanil Baaks. No artisan level before 8th character level. Living rules: XP cost paid, materials paid by the Almas academy, but the hero sits out one real-time month per lightsaber (and one more per upgrade to +2 or +3), then reports to campaign staff and gets a certificate; transfer needs Jedi Council (directors) approval. The application lists stats, Force feats, ranks, FP and DSP history, plus a 1,000-word essay on four questions. Due May 31, 2003; Jae Walker and staff announce at Origins 2003. p2 OCR was empty and read from crop /tmp/c433.jpg; pp1-2 |

An anonymous Holonet interview with 'X', a member of a local trade organization, about Burnout, a Thaereian space station trading in spice, chems, weapons, information (Bothans), slaves and exotic animals. Thaere's government is its navy; Tramsig is the real power; the navy invests rather than polices ('darksiders'); X claims 173,000 soldiers against a charter of 10,000; Cularin citizens may be sold through Burnout; Nirama likely knows and is biding his time. Trilogy box on p2 (Nirama). p3 OCR was empty and read from crop /tmp/c434.jpg; pp1-3 |
| d435 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/06:19:03 - Devan.pdf | 2 | 1-2 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 11 | 1 | 2 | 1-2: 06:19:03 - Devan (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-2: hand-read (full text): Living Force web article: 'Devan', by Morrie Mullins (former Plot Director), with a spoiler warning for the WF2003 'Forces of Cularin'. Jedi Master Devan For'deschel was the first outsider to reach Cularin after the darkstaff events. After Kirlocca's loss the Council appointed her Mistress of Lightsabers at Almas. She teaches defensive Djem So, called 'Form V' in the text; Kirlocca was credited with Shii-Cho (Form I). Game note: only Form I and V feats are available by default in LF. She was trained by Mace Windu. Quotes on the role of the Jedi, Cularin, Lanius, and being a 'Master'. Full stats (Jedi Consular 14/Jedi Master 2); pp1-2 |

| d438 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/08:07:03 - The Darkstaff.pdf | 2 | 1-2 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 12 | 0 | 2 | 1-2: 08:07:03 - The Darkstaff (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-2: hand-read (full text): Living Force web supplement for Recursion (part 3 of the 'Belted In' trilogy): 'The Darkstaff', by Morrie Mullins (former Plot Director), with a spoiler warning for 'Philology'. Recap: Len Markus took the darkstaff from the Belt and Cularin vanished. Jedi researchers recovered two tomes of Darth Rivan's journals from the Sith fortress beneath Almas; this is a HoloNet-released excerpt. Rivan on the natives who lost hyperspace travel, darkness as an ally, Almas thawing (kaluthin), and the darkstaff as an absence that feeds on the Force (1 m long, about 4 cm across, made by a forebear by accident, 'The thing wants'). The 'afterimage of a scream', his resolve to build defenses and destroy it: 'If I am to live forever, there is no other way.' Trilogy box on p1; pp1-2 |
| d439 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/08:21:03 - Thurm Loogg Speaks.pdf | 2 | 1-2 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 14 | 0 | 2 | 1-2: 08:21:03 - Thurm Loogg Speaks (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-2: hand-read (full text): Living Force web article: 'Thurm Loogg Speaks', by Morrie Mullins (former Plot Director). Loogg succeeded the late Velin Wir after the ch'hala grove affair. A Cartel broadcast hijacks every device in Cularin through a holonet security breach and 'helpfully' lists what it would never do: use Governor Chistor's financial data, sell decrypted Jedi academy traffic, or read Nirama's reports on gaps in the Thaereian perimeter. He boasts of heroes visiting Caarimon, the droid-effigy celebration, and says to defend against Sith ('there has never been a Caarite Sith Lord'), Dooku's droids and maddened gundarks. The Cartel backs Palpatine and moves goods 'with bribes and smuggling.' The feed is cut mid-'May the For--'; pp1-2 |

Yara Grugara moderates a panel on Thaere's protectorate: Colonel Hyx Modant (Bothan, Thaereian Navy, sent in Tramsig's place); Osten Dal'Nay (Militia naval commander, called 'traitor'); Sa'arli (Tarasin aide to Senator Wren) on how a protectorate is revoked; Minos (Jedi Knight); Sgt. Yadfre (Trandoshan, OPS). Yara apologizes for her funeral remarks. Footage shows Modant seizing cargo; he shoots the screen and leaves; Yadfre vomits. The p2 trilogy box was garbled and read from crop /tmp/c440.jpg; pp1-4 |
| d441 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/09:11:03 - You Are Not a Jedi Yet.pdf | 4 | 1-4 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 2 | 0 | 4 | 1-4: 09:11:03 - You Are Not a Jedi Yet (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-4: hand-read (full text): Wizards web feature 'You Are Not a Jedi Yet' by JD Wiker (Jedi Counseling author). Covers Jedi roleplaying: leading and following, areas of expertise, responsibility and consequences (submit to rightful authorities such as Cularin Sector police), using the Force and Vitality Points, Force Points and DSPs, Jedi in combat, and combat tactics (Total Defense, Fight Defensively, Disarm, Trip, Attack an Object, Ready). The GMing section has a three-strike DSP system and Living Force consequences ending in a report to Lanius Qel-Bertuk at the Almas Academy. Epigraph quotes from Qui-Gon and Yoda; pp1-4 |
| d442 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/09:18:03 - News of the Wyrd.pdf | 3 | 1-3 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 10 | 2 | 3 | 1-3: 09:18:03 - News of the Wyrd (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-3: hand-read (full text): Living Force web feature 'News of the Wyrd' by Morrie Mullins (Former Living Force Plot Director and Campaign Designer). In a Yara Grugara broadcast on the Wyrd, Force-using Tarasin witches of the Cularin jungles, a 'Hypothetical Dramatic Situation' has Raouul in a bush costume. She covers Liriana, sister of Mother Dariana, and what her Jedi friend says about balance in the Force. A montage of past Living Force events plays. Game notes give stats for Kua and Amasi. The OCR spells Liriana as 'Lirlana' once; pp1-3 |
| d443 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/10:02:03 - Cularin at Night.pdf | 2 | 1-2 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 11 | 0 | 2 | 1-2: 10:02:03 - Cularin at Night (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-2: hand-read (full text): Living Force scenario supplement for Night's Friend (part 2 of the 'Night Eyes' trilogy): 'Cularin at Night', by Morrie Mullins. Opens with graffiti verse from a Gadrin warehouse, then an anonymous informant's monologue. Topics: old Ezil; the Wyrd dismissed; Thaereian night patrols ignoring OPS; the disappeared (Vad Kirn, Horis Byrla, Anistia Qu, Rof Barrges, Ezil, Nim'Ri); stun-baton 'twitch' contests and the arrest droid cart; crime figures down; Senator Wren and the Reidi Artom rumor; the form runaround; Jex monitoring the Hedrett warehouse. Holonet transcription footer. The trilogy summary box runs into p2 body text in the OCR; pp1-2 |
| d444 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/10:23:03 - Conversations with Lanius.pdf | 4 | 1-4 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 28 | 0 | 4 | 1-4: 10:23:03 - Conversations with Lanius (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-4: hand-read (full text): Living Force web feature 'Conversations With Lanius' by Morrie Mullins. A free-form interview with Jedi Master Lanius Qel-Bertuk at the Almas academy. Yara Grugara joins in violet Jedi-style robes. Topics: her memorial-service criticism and apology; her third-person speech; the Sith rule of two; Thaere (no policy, bound to the Senate, no evidence); why Yara is a journalist; Lanius's speech on meaning and the living Force; frailty. Ends with Yara's 'are you allowed to date?' The OCR mislabels LQB as 'LGB' twice; pp1-4 |
| d445 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/11:06:03 - Thaere is Not Your Friend.pdf | 3 | 1-3 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 19 | 0 | 3 | 1-3: 11:06:03 - Thaere is Not Your Friend (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-3: hand-read (full text): Living Force scenario supplement for Night's Homecoming (conclusion of the 'Night Eyes' trilogy): 'Thaere Is Not Your Friend', by Morrie Mullins. TINYF graffiti and merchandise. On 'Eye on Cularin', Ryk Osentay notes missing reporter Melanda Forswoth. Guest 1 is a stick-wielding fake 'evil overlord' who gets stunned. Guest 2 works in surveillance and reveals a 200 million credit IBC transfer to the Thaereian Navy, IBC account back-doors, and Thaere working with the Separatists. Guest 2 is unmasked as Gerta Haman and beaten by Thaereian guards. Next day, the ship taking Haman to Coruscant (with Jedi Knight Ish-Bel Tur aboard) explodes. Short lines the OCR dropped ('Guest 2: No . . .' on p2; 'Ryk: "We"?' on p3) were read from crops /tmp/c445.jpg and /tmp/c445b.jpg; pp1-3 |
| d446 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/11:20:03 - Who is Baylan?.pdf | 3 | 1-3 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 12 | 2 | 3 | 1-3: 11:20:03 - Who is Baylan? (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-3: hand-read (full text): Living Force web feature 'Who Is Baylan?' by Morrie Mullins. Profile of the slicer Baylan, with archived Holonet quotations on government, the Jedi Temple, the Senate, redistributing credits, whining, dumb questions, technology and control, and brilliance. Includes stats for Baylan (Scoundrel 8/Outlaw Slicer 10), the new feat Binary Influence, and the Slicer Lore use of Knowledge (technology). A p3 footnote says one quote comes from a Clone Wars-onset Holonet rant (Baylan sides with neither Loyalists nor Separatists); pp1-3 |

A monologue by Blackie, a street informant on Tolea Biqua: his holovid-pilot theory; Valdo and the thermal detonator; Riboga; Nirama's style; Rufus Trammel and the Chevin slaver Phylus Mon; the Blink; his street-smarts-and-spawning theory; Trammel quietly packing up; the power-pie metaphor. Includes a Dorumaa's Children summary box (Leviathan calves, one missing) that the OCR splits across pp1-2; pp1-2 |
| d448 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Articles/Year 3/12:11:03 - False Horizon.pdf | 3 | 1-3 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 16 | 0 | 3 | 1-3: 12:11:03 - False Horizon (Year 3): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-3: hand-read (full text): Living Force web feature 'False Horizon' by Morrie Mullins (carries a 'Night Eyes' spoiler warning). Osten Dal'Nay and Broof Yurdel announce Operation False Horizon, the Militia seizing Thaereian bases. Yara Grugara reports Thaereian troops pulled back into Soboll. Lanius Qel-Bertuk says the Jedi consented because communications with Coruscant were blocked. Yara names (without naming) three CCB executives arrested for conspiracy. Senator Lavina Wren's holorecording follows. Video shows a belt battle where outnumbered Militia Z-95s are saved by fighters and a freighter bearing a green-and-white 'N' insignia; pp1-3 |

A slicer holonet thread. tinyf posts a sliced Tramsig memo ('Security Classification D', to all Cularin-based Thaereian Navy EMF commanders) ordering a social and economic destabilization program. Replies: wampa1 versus myrlinator and tharestinx; OPS Corporal Jarg Fleffant (Gadrin OPS); Alyssa (alyssaroxu, 'future apprentice to Baylan', outed by wampa1 as Alysssa Maggrit, a 12-year-old Duros girl from Tolea Biqua); IMPEVERI; kelsoblod; a 0.0.0 node poster (__0__J); mstrslcr; a 1337 spoofer traced by Alyssa to Varna Biqua; aidedecamp for Senator Wren; NJ. Alyssa fries wampa1's node, and holosrv suspends the forum. User IDs are fictional in-world characters; pp1-7 |

Covers the Gadrin statue and Reidi's habit of naming systems after herself. Coordinates from the Tarasin Vriisan irstat lead to a cave. Mother Kasslan (successor to Niroida) recites words handed down. The manifesto covers life across the galaxy, the ethics of expansion and warning indigenous species, cause and effect, habit versus fad (the wood fad), depletion, and war over unowned space ('no bystanders in wars'), and ends with the short version of the creed; pp1-3 |

Jeht sends R-0 off in the Legacy, rams through the asteroid belt, catches the Shadowblade in a tractor beam and drags both ships into a Darkstaff time rift. Game notes: the web updates end and the campaign closes December 31, 2006. |

| d539 | Star Wars - Roleplaying Game Revised Core/Living Force Campaign/Campaign Guide/Character Creation Guidelines/v.6.01 - Year 5.pdf | 15 | 1-15 |  | DONE (hand-read full text) | 0 | 0 | 15 | 1-15: v.6.01 - Year 5 (Character Creation Guidelines): every page OCR-scanned for named-character power statements/feats and stat blocks; 0 candidate sentences hand-reviewed, 0 logged (no power-scaling content of note); 1-15: hand-read (full text): Living Force Hero Creation Guidelines v6.01 (Year 5, January 2005). Year 5 changes: every hero starts at 4th level (6,000 XP); replacements for dead heroes above 8th level start at half level. 'With the Clone Wars raging' the Jedi Order takes no new students, so no new Jedi PCs. Species now come from the Ultimate Alien Anthology; Hero's Guide prestige classes and feats added; Jedi Master prestige class waived to Jedi Knight. Trials: unatoned DSP blocks Knighthood; no red lightsabers. Equipment: new heroes start with 4,500 cr; long AEG restricted list; droid, vehicle and starship purchase lists with class discounts. Force Adept apprentices and Jedi mentors retired for plot reasons (citing the 'Conversation in Shadow' article, July 28, 2004). Named favor-granters: Senator Wren, Governor Barnab Chistor, Westa Impeveri, Master Lanius Qel-Bertuk, Colonel Tramsig, Nirama. New house rules (Far Shot, Hero's Guide, small lightsabers, Living Force master spy, Force skill rank caps, Move Object, income, two-handed AOO). Appendices: A crafting and modification; B permits (CLRB, legal and forged cost tables; p14 tables read from the page image); C favors. No quotes. |
